such that
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1
we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{2}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|,|\tilde{\mathsf{J}}_{3}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|\leq C(\|g\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{-1},

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|,|\tilde{\mathsf{J}}_{2}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|\leq C(\|g\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{-2},

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathsf{J}}_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|\leq C(\|g\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{-3}.

Before starting with the proof of the main lemmas of the Appendix, we will collect a couple of auxiliary results. In first place, we note that for any
g\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}),
an inequality that we will repeatedly apply in our arguments is the following:

(134)

|\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{i}g]({\bf x})|\leq|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2-i}\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\qquad i=1,2.

Note that the above reduces to the Sobolev embedding
H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and
H^{2,1}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C(D_{\varepsilon})
.
Secondly, we obtain an uniform bound for the difference of the auxiliary functions
\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]
and their derivatives in terms of the
H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
-norm of the difference
\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}.

Lemma 7.3
.

Let
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
with
0<\delta(\varepsilon)\ll 1
small enough. The following bounds hold

(1)

|(\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2i}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2},\qquad i=1,2.

(2)

|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2i}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2},\qquad i=1,2.

(3)

|(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2(i+1)}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2},\qquad i=1,2.

Proof of
i)
.

Using some trigonometric identities for hyperbolic functions
\sinh(\cdot),\cosh(\cdot)
we get

\displaystyle\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\cosh(\bar{y})\left[\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]}{\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\cos(\bar{x})\sinh(\bar{y})\left[\cosh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]}{\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]},

and

\displaystyle\left(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\cos(\bar{x})\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]}{\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]}.

Notice that both denominators vanish if and only if
\bar{{\bf x}}=0
. Now, since
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{2}(D_{\varepsilon})
are continuous functions on a bounded domain, the outer region is easily bounded by the required term. Then, we just focus our attention on the inner region
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1
.

Applying Taylor expansion of each trigonometric function involved we obtain that each numerator of the previous expression can be bound as follows

\left|\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\cosh(\bar{y})\left[\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\sinh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]\right|\\
\leq C(\varepsilon)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{3}\frac{1}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}},

\left|\cos(\bar{x})\sinh(\bar{y})\left[\cosh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]\right|\\
\leq C(\varepsilon)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{3}\frac{\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}},

and

\left|\cos(\bar{x})\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))\right]\right|\\
\leq C(\varepsilon)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\frac{\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}.

Combining all with the usual lower bound for the denominator (see (
131
)), we obtain

|(\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2j}\leq\left(\frac{C(\varepsilon,\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{3}},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})}\right)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

Finally, as
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})),
taking
0<\delta<\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})^{-1}
we have proved our goal.
∎

Proof of
ii)
.

Due to the relations

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{2}[g]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[g])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{1}[g]-\Psi_{1}[g]\Psi_{2}[g])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x}),

it is clear that adding and subtracting some appropriate term we obtain the expressions

(135)

\displaystyle(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])^{2})({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}),

and

(136)

\displaystyle(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}).

As before, we just focus on the inner region
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1,
which is the most singular part. Now, remembering (
134
) and applying repeatedly Corollary
7.1
, we get for
1\leq i\leq 2
and
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1
that

|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})||\bar{{\bf x}}|^{i}\\
\lesssim\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}+|\bar{{\bf x}}||(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|+|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}|(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|.

Finally, applying i) in each of the last two terms of the above expressions we have proved ii).
∎

Proof of iii).

Taking one derivative on (
135
), (
136
) and adding and subtracting appropriate terms, we obtain the expressions

\displaystyle(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-3\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]+2\Psi_{1}^{3}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{x}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\partial_{\bf x}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\quad-(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x}),

and

\displaystyle(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-2\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])(1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}](\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}](\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}.

The computations are very long and tedious but share lot of similarities. For this reason we shall focus only on
(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
to illustrate how the estimates work. The ideas is to write the above long expressions in groups of terms as

•

(1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-2\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})),

•

(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})),

•

(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])\left(-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right),

•

(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}),

•

(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])(-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})).

Remembering (
134
) and applying repeatedly Corollary
7.1
, we get for
1\leq i\leq 2
and
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1
that

\displaystyle|(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{i}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})||\bar{{\bf x}}|^{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{-1}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}+|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|

\displaystyle\quad+|\bar{{\bf x}}||(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|+|\bar{{\bf x}}||(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|

\displaystyle\quad+|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|.

Finally, applying i) and ii) in each of the terms of the above expressions we have proved iii).
∎

At this point, we have all the ingredients to prove the main lemmas of the Appendix. The first one allows us to take derivatives into the kernel
\partial_{{\bf x}}^{i}K[g]
as follows:

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
g\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
with
0<\delta(\varepsilon)\ll 1
small enough. The following bounds hold

(1)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\sup_{\bar{{\bf x}}\in D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}),

(2)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|\partial_{{\bf x}}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}),

(3)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}),

(4)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}).

Proof.

As we can see the computations share lot of similarities. For this reason we shall focus only on one significant term iii) to illustrate how the estimates work. Remembering (
132
) we get

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\mathsf{I}_{i}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}

Proceeding as usual, we split the integral into inner and outer regions. As
g\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{2}(D_{\varepsilon})
is a continuous function on a bounded domain the outer integral is trivially bounded by some universal constant. To handle the remaining inner integral
\{({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\in D_{\varepsilon}\times D_{\varepsilon}(y):|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1\}
of each term, we use Corollary
7.2
as follows:

\mathsf{I}_{1})

As
g\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
we have that
\partial_{{\bf x}}g\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and consequently we get

\displaystyle\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\mathsf{I}_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}

\displaystyle=\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}\left|\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x})\right|^{4}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right),

\mathsf{I}_{2})

As
g\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
we have that
\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}g\in H^{2,1}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C(D_{\varepsilon})
and consequently we get

\displaystyle\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\mathsf{I}_{2}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}

\displaystyle=\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{2}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}\left|\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}g]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}

\displaystyle\leq C(\delta,\|g\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\frac{1}{|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2(1-\gamma)}}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right).

As
0<\gamma<1,
the last term is integrable and we have proved our goal. The rest of the terms i), ii) and iv) work in a similar way and we omit the details.
∎

Now, we continue with an analog result for the difference
\partial_{{\bf x}}^{i}K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{i}K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]
.

Lemma 7.5
.

Let
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
with
0<\delta(\varepsilon)\ll 1
small enough. The following bounds hold

(1)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\sup_{\bar{{\bf x}}\in D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

(2)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

(3)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

(4)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

Proof of
i)
.

We start by remembering the kernel definition
\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]=K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]
given by

\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\log\left[\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right].

Adding and subtracting some appropriate term, one finds

\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}=1+\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})},

and using the standard logarithmic inequality
1-x^{-1}\leq\log x\leq x-1
for all
x>0
, we get the lower and upper bounds

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}.

As
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
are arbitrary functions we can assume without loss of generality that

|\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|\leq\left|\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|,

and using the trigonometric identity
\cosh(a+b)=\cosh(a)\cosh(b)+\sinh(a)\sinh(b)
we finally get

\displaystyle\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}

\displaystyle=\cosh(\bar{y})\frac{\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sinh(\bar{y})\frac{\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}.

Now, we will use the Taylor expansion of the trigonometric functions
\cosh(\cdot)
and
\sinh(\cdot)
together with the algebraic identity
a^{n}-b^{n}=(a-b)\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}a^{k}b^{n-1-k}
to obtain

\displaystyle\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{2n-1}\frac{\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{k}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{2n-1-k}}{(2n)!},

\displaystyle\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{2n}\frac{\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{k}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{2n-k}}{(2n+1)!}.

Computing the infinite sum of the geometric series
\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}r^{2n}
with

r:=\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon}),

we finally get (under condition
\delta<\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})^{-1}
) that

\displaystyle\left|\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\frac{\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})},

\displaystyle\left|\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)-\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon)|\bar{{\bf x}}|\frac{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}}{1-\max\{\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\}\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})},

where we have applied the Sobolev embedding
L^{\infty}(D_{\varepsilon})\hookrightarrow H^{2}(D_{\varepsilon}).

Now, it is not difficult to see that the following uniform bound holds

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\sup_{\bar{{\bf x}}\in D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\frac{\cosh(\bar{y})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}+\sinh(\bar{y})|\bar{{\bf x}}|}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|^{2}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon)

thanks to the fact (see (
131
)) that for
|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1
we have

|\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})|\geq\left(\tfrac{1}{2}-\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\right)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}-C(\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{4}.

Then, taking
0<\delta<\text{diam}(D_{\varepsilon})^{-1}
small enough and combining all we have proved our goal.
∎

Proof of
ii)
.

As
\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
and
\partial_{{\bf x}}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\Psi_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x})
, adding and subtracting some appropriate term we obtain

(137)

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}).

To bound the first term we use
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
, which implies
\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
. As usual, splitting the integral and using Corollary
7.1
in the inner region we obtain directly that

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\\
\leq C(\varepsilon,\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

For the second term of (
137
), using that
\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and applying Lemma
7.3
we get

\displaystyle\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})},\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

Finally, as the above holds for any
{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}
, combining the above and taking the supremum over all the domain we have proved the desired inequality.
∎

Proof of
iii)
.

Taking a derivative of (
137
) we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

(138)

\displaystyle\quad+(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}).

To bound the first term of (
7
), as
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
we get
\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
. Now, splitting the integral and using (
129
) together with Corollary
7.1
in the inner region we obtain

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq\frac{C(\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}{1-C(\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

To bound the second term of (
7
) we use the same type of ideas. As
\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
we get
\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})\in H^{2,1}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C(D_{\varepsilon})
. Now, splitting the integral and applying Corollary
7.1
in the inner region we obtain

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\left|\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\frac{C(\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}{1-C(\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}\left(\int_{|\bar{{\bf x}}|\ll 1}\frac{1}{|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2(1-\gamma)}}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right),

where the last integral is bounded thanks to the fact that
0<\gamma<1.
Finally, to bound the last two terms of (
7
) we use the fact that
\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and
\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\in H^{2,1}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C(D_{\varepsilon})
to get

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2(1+\gamma)}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\,

and

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}.

Using auxiliary Lemma
7.3
on the last terms of the above expressions we have proved our goal.
∎

Proof of
iv)
.

As before, taking a derivative of (
7
) we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+2\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})

\displaystyle\quad+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+2\left(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})

(139)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})+\left(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}).

In first place, due to the relations (
129
) and (
130
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]=[\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}](1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])-2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}](\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])]\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}])\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}].

Remembering (
134
) and applying repeatedly Corollary
7.1
, we get that the first three terms of (
7
) can be handle in the following way:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3-i}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{i}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}^{2}.

The remaining three terms of (
7
) follows by auxiliary Lemma
7.3
. Notice that using (
134
) we get

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\sup_{\bar{{\bf x}}\in D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x},

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x},

and

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\leq\|\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|(\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{4}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}.

Using auxiliary Lemma
7.3
on the last terms of the above expressions we have proved our goal.
∎

Lemma 7.6
.

Let
\mathsf{f}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon}))
with
0<\delta(\varepsilon)\ll 1
small enough and
h\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
with
\|h\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}=1.
For
0<\tau\ll 1
, the following bound holds

(1)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\sup_{\bar{{\bf x}}\in D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|^{2}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

(2)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\right|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

(3)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

(4)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

Proof of
i)
.

Just applying the standard logarithmic inequality
1-x^{-1}\leq\log x\leq x-1
for all
x>0
, we get the lower and upper bounds

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}.

Therefore, we have

\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|\\
\leq\max_{0\leq\xi\leq\tau}\left|\frac{1}{\tau}\left(\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right)-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|.

Now, using the trigonometric identity
\cosh(a+b)=\cosh(a)\cosh(b)+\sinh(a)\sinh(b)
on the above expression, we get

\frac{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\\
=\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))\frac{\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-1}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\\
\quad+\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))\frac{\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})},

and consequently

\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|\\
\leq\left|\frac{\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-1}{\tau}\right|\max_{0\leq\xi\leq\tau}\left|\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|\\
+\left|\frac{\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|\max_{0\leq\xi\leq\tau}\left|\frac{\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|.

Applying the Taylor expansion of the trigonometric functions
\sinh(\cdot),\cosh(\cdot)
together with the fact that
\|h\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}=1
we get the bounds

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\cosh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)-1}{\tau}\right|\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sinh\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\tau h]({\bf x})\right)}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{3}.

In addition, we have

\max\left\{\left|\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|,\left|\frac{\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}](x))}{\cosh\left(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\xi h]({\bf x})\right)-\cos(\bar{x})}\right|\right\}\\
\leq\frac{C(\|\mathsf{f}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})}{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}-\|\mathsf{f}+\xi h\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\right)|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}-C(\|\mathsf{f}+\xi h\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{4}},

which give us

\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|\leq\max_{0\leq\xi\leq\tau}\frac{C(\varepsilon,\|\mathsf{f}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\tau}{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}-\|\mathsf{f}+\xi h\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})}\right)-C(\|\mathsf{f}+\xi h\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}}.

Coming back to the starting point, as
\mathsf{f}\in\mathbb{B}_{\delta}(X(D_{\varepsilon}))
and
\|h\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}=1,
taking
0<\delta(\varepsilon),\tau\ll 1
small enough we have proved our desired inequality.
∎

Proof of
ii)
.

As
\mathcal{K}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\left(K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-K[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
and
\partial_{{\bf x}}K[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\Psi_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x})
, adding and subtracting some appropriate term we obtain

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime},\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}(\mathsf{f}^{\prime}-\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime})]({\bf x})+\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime\prime}]({\bf x}).

So, it is just a matter of algebra to get

(140)

\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}=\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

where both new functionals are given respectively by

(141)

\displaystyle\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=\left[\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x}).

Directly from auxiliary Lemma
7.3
, as
h\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
implies
\partial_{{\bf x}}h\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and by hypothesis
\|h\|_{H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})}=1,
we get

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]\right|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

To obtain something similar for
\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]
we need to work a little bit more. In first place, thanks to the definition of
\Psi_{1}[\cdot]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
, adding and subtracting some appropriate term we have

\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\frac{\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})}\\
-\sinh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]},

and using on it some trigonometric identities, we obtain

\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\left[\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\cosh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\right]\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})-\cos(\bar{x})}\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\cosh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right).

Now, dividing the above expression by
\tau
and subtracting the term
(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})
we obtain that

\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\\
=\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\frac{\sinh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\cosh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))\frac{\sinh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x})-\cos(\bar{x})}\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\cosh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right).

Finally, using the Taylor expansion of the trigonometric functions
\sinh(\cdot),\cosh(\cdot)
and (
134
) give us
the desired inequality

(142)

\sup_{{\bf x}\in D_{\varepsilon}}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]\right|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

∎

Proof of iii).

Recalling (
140
), we have

(143)

\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}=\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

where, using relation
\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[g]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}g]({\bf x})
whit
\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[g]=\left(\Psi_{2}[g]-(\Psi_{1}[g])^{2}\right)
, we get

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
+\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})-\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\\
+\frac{(\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}-\left(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}),

and

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
+\tau\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\right)^{2}.

By definition of
\tilde{\mathsf{I}}_{1}[\cdot]
and relations (
129
) and (
130
), the previous expressions can be rewritten in a more manageable way as

(144)

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{A}_{1}^{\prime}+\mathsf{A}_{2}^{\prime}+\mathsf{A}_{3}^{\prime}+\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}+\mathsf{A}_{5}^{\prime})[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

(145)

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{B}_{1}^{\prime}+\mathsf{B}_{2}^{\prime}+\mathsf{B}_{3}^{\prime}+\mathsf{B}_{4}^{\prime})[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

with

\displaystyle\mathsf{A}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=\left[\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{A}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{A}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=\left[\frac{(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right]\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2},

\mathsf{A}_{5}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}):=\bigg[-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\\
+2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\bigg]\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2},

and

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=\tau(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\right)^{2},

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle:=(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x}).

Note that the only new type of term that appear is
\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h].
The rest of terms can be easily handle using auxiliary Lemma
7.3
, Corollary
7.1
and (
134
).
To sum up, our proof reduces to check that

(146)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon,\|\mathsf{f}\|_{H^{3}(D_{\varepsilon})})\tau^{2}.

By (
134
), as
\mathsf{f}\in H^{4,3}(D_{\varepsilon})
we have that
\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}\in H^{3,2}(D_{\varepsilon})\subset C^{1}(D_{\varepsilon})
and we just need to prove the same type of estimate for the term

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\frac{(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{4+2\gamma}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}.

Thanks to the definition of
\Psi_{2}[\cdot]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
, adding and subtracting some appropriate term we have

\left(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})}\\
-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]\left[\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})\right]},

and using on it some trigonometric identities, we obtain

\left(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\left[\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\cosh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\right]\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\sinh^{2}(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))+2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\sinh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))\sinh^{2}\left(\tfrac{\tau}{2}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+2\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left[1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\cosh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))\right]\sinh^{2}\left(\tfrac{\tau}{2}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left[\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})}\right]^{2}.

Now, dividing the above expression by
\tau
and subtracting the term
(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})
we obtain that

\frac{(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\left(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]\right)({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\\
=\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\frac{\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\cosh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\frac{\sinh\left(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)}{\tau}-\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\sinh^{2}(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))+2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\sinh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))\sinh^{2}\left(\tfrac{\tau}{2}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+2\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left[1-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\cosh(\tau\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x}))\right]\sinh^{2}\left(\tfrac{\tau}{2}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right)\\
+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left[\frac{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]({\bf x}))-\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))}{\cosh(\bar{y}+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}))-\cos(\bar{x})}\right]^{2}.

Finally, using the Taylor expansion of the trigonometric functions
\sinh(\cdot),\cosh(\cdot)
and (
134
) give us
the desired inequality (
146
).
∎

Proof of iv).

Recalling (
143
) together with (
144
) and (
145
), we have

(147)

\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}=\sum_{i=1}^{5}\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{i}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\sum_{i=1}^{4}\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{i}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

where,

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\\
+\left[\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right]\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\\
:=\mathsf{A}_{11}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{12}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
:=\mathsf{A}_{21}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{22}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{23}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=-(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
:=\mathsf{A}_{31}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{32}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{33}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{34}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\\
=\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
\quad+\left[\frac{(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right]2\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\\
:=\mathsf{A}_{41}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{42}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{5}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\partial_{{\bf x}}\bigg\{-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\\
+2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}](\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\bigg\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
+\bigg[-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\\
+2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}](\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\bigg]2\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\\
:=\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{52}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

and

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
+(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left[\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})+\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\right]\\
=:\mathsf{B}_{11}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{12}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=-(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\\
-(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]+\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
=:\mathsf{B}_{21}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{22}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{23}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{24}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=\tau(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
+\tau(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})2\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}h]({\bf x})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
=:\mathsf{B}_{31}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{32}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=(\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}h]({\bf x})\\
+(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}h]({\bf x})\\
=:\mathsf{B}_{41}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{42}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}).

Taking into account all the above we have proved that

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}^{3}\left\{\frac{\mathcal{K}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h,\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\\
\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{5}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{i}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{i}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x},

where each of the terms that appear can be decomposed as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{A}_{11}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{12}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{A}_{21}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{22}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{23}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{A}_{31}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{32}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{33}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{34}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{A}_{41}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{42}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}_{5}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{52}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

and

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{B}_{11}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{12}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{2}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{B}_{21}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{22}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{23}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{24}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{3}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{B}_{31}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{32}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{B}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{B}_{41}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{B}_{42}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}}).

For the sake of brevity, we shall present here the complete details for the term
\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})
and the other terms can be dealt using the previous lemmas via straightforward variations. To sum up, our goal reduces to check that

(148)

\int_{D_{\varepsilon}}\left(\int_{D_{\varepsilon}(y)}\left|\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right|^{2}|\bar{{\bf x}}|^{2}\mbox{d}\bar{{\bf x}}\right)\mbox{d}{\bf x}\leq C(\varepsilon)\tau^{2}.

Notice that
\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]
can be written in a more manageable way (adding and subtracting terms) as

\mathsf{A}_{51}^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})=2(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
-2\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
-2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}.

Now, each one of the above terms can be easily handled. Using (
134
) and applying repeatedly Lemma
7.3
we get that the first term is bounded as required. For the second term, we only need to note that it can be written, remembering (
141
), as

2\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
=2\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}).

Combining (
129
) with Corollary
7.2
and (
134
) we obtain the bound
2|\partial_{{\bf x}}\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})|\leq C(\varepsilon).
After that, as an immediate consequence of (
142
) we get the required bound for the second term. For the latter, we proceed in the same spirit as before, trying to split that term into previously studied terms. Notice that

2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\partial_{{\bf x}}\left\{\frac{(\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}+\tau h]-\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})}{\tau}-(\Psi_{2}[\mathsf{f}]-\Psi_{1}^{2}[\mathsf{f}])({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[h]({\bf x})\right\}\left(\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\right)^{2}\\
=2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})-2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\mathsf{A}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}^{2}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})\\
=2\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\left\{\mathsf{A}_{1}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{4}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})+\mathsf{A}_{5}^{\prime}[\mathsf{f},h]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\right\}\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x}).

Using Corollary
7.1
and (
134
) we obtain the bound
2|\Psi_{1}[\mathsf{f}]({\bf x},\bar{{\bf x}})\Delta_{\bar{{\bf x}}}[\partial_{{\bf x}}\mathsf{f}]({\bf x})|\leq C(\varepsilon)
. Finally, working as we did to get inequality (
146
) we get the required bound for each of the above terms. Therefore, combining all we have proved our goal.
∎
